  timed-out suite kills the whole B2/compiler process group instead of leaving
  orphaned `cppgm++` children. Verified with a forced one-second

  `+1.14%`, footprint `+0.15%`.

## Local Gate Repairs

- 2026-06-30 PA36 macOS hosted deque crash: fixed the level-0 machine-IR
  callee-saved frame recomputation so callee-save slots stay within the
  reserved frame when hidden fixed-frame bytes remain after local cleanup. The
  failure reduced to a PA28 behavior test where the old backend restored a
  preserved pointer from a nested call return address. Validation: focused PA28
  reducer, focused PA36 `700-hosted-deque-move-assign-link-smoke.t`, full PA28
  and PA38 suites, placement audit, text-reparse audit, full direct-LowIR
  `test-report` passed `3376/3376`, full direct-LowIR `test-strict` passed, and
  perf against
  `/tmp/cppgm-boost-frontier-pr36-f0124b911-20260630-perf-baseline.json` passed
  with instructions `+0.26%`, RSS `-0.39%`, footprint `+0.00%`.
- `8bb9d6213`: moved the binary-literal extension coverage out of PA6 and into
  the PA33 host-compatibility preprocessor suite. The preprocessor now reports
  `__has_feature(__cxx_binary_literals__)` and
  `__has_extension(__cxx_binary_literals__)` as supported, matching the hosted
  binary integer literal and binary UDL support added for Boost.Asio. The old
  PA6 `0b01000010` fixture was replaced by a plain invalid-token balanced-scan
  reducer using `@`, preserving coverage for the `RecogTokenBuffer` invalid
  token EOF sentinel without assigning the hosted binary-literal extension to
  PA6. Validation: `make -C dev cppgm++ preproc recog -j12`; focused PA6
  reducer; full `make -C pa6 test CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; focused PA33
  binary-literal feature and tokenization checks; `ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa33'
  ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=12 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1
  CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 make test-report-nobuild` passed `301/301`; direct
  Boost.Config `no_cxx14_binary_literals_fail.cpp` now fails quickly via the
  expected `#error`, while `no_cxx14_binary_literals_pass.cpp` compiles and
  runs with status `0`.
- `dcdd5d3e6`: restored the local lowir-compare gates before continuing Boost
  survey triage. Static hosted `typeid(T)` now uses an already requested local
  RTTI definition instead of an external ABI alias, fixing the PA34
  `std::function<int()>` link failure for `_ZTIPFivE` and the PA27 direct-text
  typeid drift. The temporary PA6 binary-literal reference refresh from this
  commit was corrected in `8bb9d6213`; the PA29 reference was refreshed for
  current `unwind=no` constructor metadata. Validation:
  focused `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa6 pa27 pa29 pa34'`
  report passed `229/229`; full `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 make
  test-report` passed `3070/3070`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 make
  test-strict` passed.
- `e20ff04b4`: temporary local direct-LowIR gate refresh after the typed NTTP
  regression. Before the witness-output repair, the PA19
  `100-negative-nontype-substitution-comparison.t` reference was aligned to the
  then-current non-odr-used static-member output. Full
  `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ... make test-report-nobuild` passed
  `3076/3076`; the reference churn was superseded by `fbde86ce6`.
- `fbde86ce6`: repaired the PA19 direct-LowIR/witness drift from source-capture
  replay of unrequired static const integral members. Witness member-value
  dependencies are now tracked on `ValueBinding`; output suppresses only
  witness-only unrequired non-`value` static const integral members when there
  is no real member-output requirement, preserving canonical trait `value`
  globals and explicit specializations. The PA19
  `100-negative-nontype-substitution-comparison.t` reference stdout is empty
  again. Validation: witness/no-witness diffs are clean for the PA19 typed NTTP
  reducer, PA19 dependent conversion, PA19 explicit specialization, two PA21
  structured-value regressions, and two PA22 static-member regressions; focused
  `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa19' ... make
  test-report-nobuild` passed `105/105`; full strict direct-LowIR compare
  passed PA18, PA19, PA21, and PA22.
- PA26 direct-LowIR reference refresh after `069681317`: the braced
  constructor argument in
  `200-direct-init-single-braced-constructor-argument.t` now materializes
  directly in the target argument slot rather than through an extra temporary
  and `copyobj`. Focused PA26 check passes; focused
  `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa26' ... make
  test-report-nobuild` passes `99/99`; full direct-LowIR report passes
  `3077/3077`.
- `84dcf6840`: repaired the Boost.Bind `bind_mf2_test` typed member-function
  pointer overload path. Dependent class-template metadata now keeps structured
  semantic arguments authoritative when stale source text names a different
  template parameter, while only the narrow single-type-parameter display case
  updates printable dependent class names. PA28 LowIR refs were refreshed for
  the earlier const member-function-pointer mangling correction (`M...KF...`).
  Validation: focused Boost-shaped reducers pass; PA28 course regression
  passes; PA28 direct-LowIR report passes `50/50`; PA22 direct-LowIR report
  passes `742/742`; full strict direct-LowIR compare passes; full
  `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ... make test-report-nobuild` passes
  `3100/3100`; focused Boost `bind_mf2_test` passes; perf check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `-0.00%`, RSS `-1.06%`, footprint `+1.41%`).
- `c96b3232a`: repaired the Boost.Bind `bind_function_ap_test` runtime crash.
  `static_cast<T&&>(value)` now preserves the original object reference when
  the target is a direct same-object class reference, instead of considering a
  user-defined conversion function such as `std::auto_ptr<T>::operator
  auto_ptr<U>()`. Validation: no-STL and `auto_ptr` forwarding reducers run
  successfully; PA22 regression passes in normal and direct-LowIR modes; PA22
  reports pass `441/441`; full strict direct-LowIR compare passes; full
  `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ... make test-report-nobuild` passes
  `3101/3101`; focused Boost `bind_function_ap_test` passes; perf check
  against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.29%`, RSS `+1.97%`, footprint `+1.37%`).
- `6c5d1adc5`: repaired the Boost.Bind `bind_nested_rv_test` compile frontier.
  `boost::shared_ptr<int> p; p != 0` exposed that integral null pointer
  constants were not considered convertible to `nullptr_t`, so the viable
  `operator!=(shared_ptr<T> const&, nullptr_t)` overload was rejected. The
  conversion layer now accepts and retags that standard conversion, and overload
  tie-breaking compares source arguments for ordinary standard conversions
  while still comparing the post-conversion result for the second standard
  conversion in user-defined conversion sequences. Validation: PA12 conversion
  and ambiguity reducers pass; PA15 operator reducer passes in normal and
  direct-LowIR modes; PA12/PA15 report passes `267/267`; PA16 direct-LowIR
  report passes `139/139`; full strict direct-LowIR compare passes; full
  direct-LowIR report passes `3104/3104`; focused Boost `bind_nested_rv_test`
  compiles and advances to a separate link-time missing
  `boost::shared_ptr<int>` move constructor; perf check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.35%`, RSS `+1.20%`, footprint `+1.36%`).
- `555f3ef01`: repaired the Boost.Bind `bind_nested_rv_test` link frontier.
  A by-value call through a function pointer,
  `function(static_cast<T&&>(value))`, needed the inline
  `boost::shared_ptr<int>` move constructor, but semantic output only collected
  hidden argument materialization support when the call target was a direct
  function binding. Indirect function-pointer calls now use the structured
  callee function type that CallSem already has, avoiding text reparsing while
  requiring the same hidden copy/move support as direct calls. The resulting
  PA18 LowIR reducer also exposed an output-on-use ordering issue where a move
  constructor could be emitted before the same complete-entry copy constructor;
  LowIR generation now builds a one-time copy-before-move prerequisite map for
  constructor definitions and uses an incremental emitted-symbol set rather
  than broad call-argument tracking. A broader runtime-reference pre-scan was
  rejected after perf regressed. Validation: no-STL reducer
  `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260526/template_inline_move_ctor_emit.cpp`
  compiles, links, and runs with exit `0`; PA18 regression
  `100-template-function-pointer-rvalue-argument-emits-move.t` passes in
  normal and direct-LowIR modes; PA18 direct-LowIR report passes `167/167`;
  full `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ... make test-report-nobuild`
  passes `3105/3105`; full strict direct-LowIR compare passes; focused Boost
  `libs/bind/test//bind_nested_rv_test` builds, links, and runs under B2; perf
  check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.18%`, RSS `+0.49%`, footprint `+1.36%`).
- `3e805a2e6`: repaired the Boost.Bind `apply_rv_test` /
  `apply_rv_test2` compile frontier. Forwarding function lvalues through
  Boost.Bind deduced argument types such as `F & (&)()`, and libc++'s
  `std::tuple<A&...>` constructor SFINAE asked
  `__is_constructible(F & (&)(), F & (&)())`. The structural builtin-trait
  evaluator treated every target whose unreferenced base was a function as an
  unconstructible function object type, even when the actual target was a
  reference. The evaluator now lets reference targets use the existing
  structured standard-conversion check, with no source reparsing. Validation:
  no-STL reducer
  `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260526/constructible_function_ref_tuple_sfinae_nostl.cpp`
  and hosted libc++ tuple reducer
  `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260526/tuple_function_reference_pack.cpp`
  compile; PA33 regression
  `771-builtin-constructible-function-ref-sfinae.t` passes; PA34 report passes
  `302/302`; full direct-LowIR report passes `3106/3106`; full strict
  direct-LowIR compare passes; focused Boost `apply_rv_test` and
  `apply_rv_test2` pass; full `JOBS=12 ./run-cppgm-b2.sh -a libs/bind/test`
  passes and updates 303 targets; perf check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.26%`, RSS `+1.16%`, footprint `+1.35%`).
- `a6da73958`: repaired the first Boost.Bloom compile frontier. Qualified
  array type template arguments such as `std::uint32_t[4]` were recognized
  neither as direct qualified type-ids nor as dependent expressions by the
  template-argument fragment classifier, so the structured `type_id` syntax was
  dropped before explicit type-argument resolution and strict fallback auditing
  stopped in `filter_core::next_element`. The parser now treats `[]` after a
  non-template qualified type name as type syntax, preserving structured type
  resolution without adding a source-text reparse path. Validation: no-STL
  reducers
  `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260526/qualified_array_type_template_arg_tiny.cpp`,
  `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260526/nested_qualified_array_type_template_arg.cpp`,
  and `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260526/bloom_array_type_arg_min.cpp` compile;
  PA18 regression
  `100-qualified-array-type-template-argument.t` passes; PA18 placement audit
  passes with `--fail-on-early`; PA18 report passes `168/168`; PA18
  direct-LowIR report passes `168/168`; full strict direct-LowIR compare
  passes; full direct-LowIR report passes `3107/3107`; focused Boost
  `libs/bloom/test//test_bulk_operations` and full `libs/bloom/test` advance to
  the next frontier, `invalid array braced-init-list` in
  `boost::mp11::detail::mp_for_each_impl`, with 9 compile targets still
  failing; perf check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.26%`, RSS `+1.98%`, footprint `+1.40%`).
- 602485622: repaired the next Boost.Bloom compile frontier. Substitution
  while probing `used_value_size<subfilter>` already had the structured bound
  type `block<unsigned int[4], 4>`, but explicit type-argument resolution later
  saw only generated text `unsigned int [4]` and hit the hard fallback audit.
  The fix keeps the lookup narrow: when the fallback path scans local

- `d0ca97c2d`: repaired the next Boost.Bloom lazy-body materialization
  frontier. Both the eager and lazy AST parsers recognized GNU `__int128`, but

  specifiers or as cast/type-id starters. Boost.Bloom's `detail::umul128`
  declares `__uint128_t r = (__uint128_t)x * y;`, so lazy header-body
  materialization rejected the local declaration and cast before semantic
  analysis. The fix extends the existing GNU int128 parser predicate to those
  typedef spellings and consults it in type-specifier-seq and type-id lookahead;
  it adds no reparse path or new tracking structure. Validation: pre-fix PA33
  lazy-header reducer, direct primary-source `__uint128_t` local reducer, and

- `5b52e9726`: repaired the next Boost.Bloom static-member overload compile
  frontier. In `filter_core::capacity_for`, the unqualified static-member call
  `used_array_size(rng)` sees both a non-static `used_array_size() const` and

- d7150a985: repaired the next Boost.Bloom explicit type-argument compile

- dffdaa46d: fixed the Boost.Bloom runtime `std::bad_alloc` frontier in
  `test_capacity`. `filter_core::capacity_for(100, 0.1)` returned an enormous
  capacity because native call lowering materialized XMM arguments sequentially:
  after `exp` returned in `xmm0`, the expression argument for `pow` lived in
  `xmm1` and the exponent lived in `xmm0`; call setup copied `xmm1` into
  `xmm0` before reading the second argument, so both `pow` arguments became the
  first value. The fix keeps this in the native backend by lowering XMM
  register arguments as a parallel copy, using a spare XMM register when one is
  available and reserving a single 16-byte call scratch slot only for all-XMM
  cycles with no spare register. Stack-passed float arguments are stored before
  the XMM shuffle, so delayed register materialization cannot corrupt them.
  Validation: focused reducer changed from `direct_pow=0.694842` to
  `direct=0.1 stored=0.1` and exits `0`; dumped MIR now uses `xmm2` to shuffle
  the `xmm1`/`xmm0` cycle before `pow`; focused PA32 regression passes; PA32
  report passes `65/65`; PA23 direct-LowIR report passes `103/103` after
  updating structural MIR refs for the intentional call-setup order/no-op
  drift; focused Boost `libs/bloom/test//test_capacity` passes; a full
  `libs/bloom/test` rerun advanced past `test_capacity` and then showed the
  next confirmed frontier in `test_construction`
  (`hash_function().state` remains `42` where Boost expects `-1`), while
  `test_fpr` became a CPU-bound long runtime and was stopped after the local
  stall cap; full direct-LowIR report passes `3114/3114`; strict direct-LowIR
  compare passes; perf check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.23%`, RSS `+0.98%`, footprint `+1.37%`).
- `3d58fd644`: fixed the next Boost.Bloom runtime state frontier in
  `test_construction`. Defaulted move construction for `filter` was classified
  with the copy-triviality predicate, so the generated `filter(filter&&)` body
  used `copyobj` for an `empty_value<Hash>` base even when the selected
  `Hash(Hash&&)` constructor was nontrivial and reset the source state. The fix
  adds a structured move-triviality query beside the existing host-ABI copy
  query, uses it only for move-constructor trivial-lifecycle decisions, and
  applies the same move-aware predicate to synthesized move-constructor trivial
  field-prefix copies. No source reparse or broad tracking table is added.
  Validation: the reduced `stateful`/`empty_value`/`filter` probe now exits `0`
  and emitted LowIR calls the nontrivial base move constructor instead of
  copying storage; focused PA16 regression passes; PA16 direct-LowIR report
  passes `140/140`; PA16 placement audit marks the new reducer owner-reached

  and updates 38 targets, including `test_fpr`; full direct-LowIR report passes

  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.28%`, RSS `+1.17%`, footprint `+1.35%`).
- `6c3d61942`: fixed the first Boost.Charconv compile frontier. The primary
  `dragon_box_print_chars<Float, FloatTraits>` function template takes
  `typename FloatTraits::carrier_uint`, while the explicit specializations in
  `to_chars.cpp` spell the resolved integer type in the parameter list. After
  explicit template arguments were substituted, the specialization matcher kept
  the structured dependent member type
  `dragonbox_float_traits<float>::carrier_uint` and compared it directly
  against `std::uint32_t`, so no function template matched and collection
  failed with `unknown function template explicit specialization`. The fix
  resolves only the substituted explicit-specialization function type before
  signature comparison, using the existing dependent-type resolver and adding
  no source reparse path or tracking table. Validation: the no-STL reducer
  failed before the fix and now compiles; PA22 regression
  `518-explicit-specialization-dependent-param-typedef.t` passes; PA22
  placement audit passes with `--fail-on-early`; PA22 direct-LowIR report
  passes `444/444`; direct Boost `to_chars.cpp` advances to
  `unsupported global array initializer for
  boost::charconv::detail::extended_cache_long_impl<true>::multiplier_index_info_table`;
  full `libs/charconv/test` rerun advances to that same next frontier with one
  failed update; full direct-LowIR report passes `3116/3116`; strict
  direct-LowIR compare passes after rerunning an isolated transient PA19 witness
  failure cleanly; perf check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.29%`, RSS `+2.07%`, footprint `+1.37%`).
- `132484e04`: fixed the next Boost.Charconv `to_chars.o` static table
  frontier. `extended_cache_long_impl<true>::multiplier_index_info_table`
  instantiated as field assignment statements into a static constexpr array of
  nested aggregate class elements, and `main_cache_holder_impl<true>::cache`
  instantiated as constructor actions for constexpr `uint128` elements. LowIR
  global collection only handled scalar braced array data, so it rejected the
  aggregate assignment shape and then the constructor-action shape as
  unsupported global array initializers. The fix stays on structured semantic
  nodes: it overlays scalar field assignment data into global arrays, and for
  simple constructor-action arrays it inspects the resolved constructor body for
  `this->field = parameter/constant` assignments and substitutes the call
  arguments into static data. It does not reparse source text or add a broad
  tracking table, and it avoids generating large dynamic initializer call
  sequences for Boost's constexpr tables. Validation: both PA20 reducers pass
  and emit static global data; PA20 placement audit passes with
  `--fail-on-early`; PA20 direct-LowIR report passes `77/77`; direct Boost
  `to_chars.cpp` compiles; full `libs/charconv/test` rerun updates 148 targets
  with `to_chars.test` passing and one remaining `from_chars.test` runtime
  failure at the signed `__int128_t` numeric-limits assertion; full
  direct-LowIR report passes `3118/3118` at low concurrency; strict
  direct-LowIR compare passes; three-run perf check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.27%`, RSS `+2.48%`, footprint `+1.37%`).
- `a6a9d8997`: fixed the Boost.Charconv `from_chars.test` signed `__int128_t`
  runtime frontier. libc++'s `numeric_limits<__int128_t>::max()` returns a
  static constexpr class member `__max`; cppgm had cached that member through
  the legacy `long long` `constant_value` field and folded it to
  `9223372036854775807`, so the Boost check against `unsigned long long` max
  failed. Simply loading the member was not valid because C++11 static
  constexpr integral members need no out-of-class storage definition for this
  use, so the fix inlines the already-structured initializer AST for int128
  static constexpr scalar members and leaves the normal non-int128 fast path
  unchanged. No source reparsing or broad tracking table is added. Validation:
  the no-STL `limits_like<__int128_t>::max()` reducer and hosted `<limits>`
  reducer fail before the fix and now run with exit `0`; PA33 regression
  `772-static-constexpr-int128-member-not-narrowed.t` passes; PA33 direct-LowIR
  report passes `305/305`; PA33 placement audit still exits nonzero on
  pre-existing PA33 cluster-early tests, with the new regression in the hosted
  int128 cluster; full `libs/charconv/test` passes and updates 150 targets
  with log `/tmp/boost-frontier-charconv-after-int128-static-constexpr-20260527.log`;
  full direct-LowIR report passes `3119/3119`; strict direct-LowIR compare
  passes; three-run perf check against
  `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json`
  passes (instructions `+0.31%`, RSS `+1.10%`, footprint `+1.37%`).

- `49ee6db36`: routed `--emit-abi-facts` through the structured ABI model

- this commit: completed the structured ABI route for member template-template
  arguments. Template argument semantics now carries a resolved member-template

  `make -C dev cppgm++ abimangle -j8`; focused PA22 reducer with

- this commit: repaired the PA35 hosted inline `thread_local` deque destructor

- this commit: repaired the 2026-06-18 Boost.Array `array0` revalidation

Local Boost wrapper state:

- `/Users/vishvananda/boost_1_91_0/cppgm-b2-toolchain.sh` points at this
  worktree's `dev/cppgm++`.
- The wrapper routes C and assembly compile/preprocess actions to host clang,
  C++ compile/preprocess actions to `cppgm++`, and link actions to host clang++.
- Runtime Boost verification should use direct Boost.Build execution. If
  freshly linked Mach-O executables stall and unified logs report
  `AppleSystemPolicy` / `Security policy would not allow process`, enable the
  launching terminal application under macOS Privacy & Security -> Developer
  Tools and restart the terminal/Codex process tree before rerunning. Do not
  substitute a checked-in launcher for normal frontier validation.

## Sequential Process

1. Run Boost suites in the order listed in
   `docs/boost-b2-suite-status-20260511.md`.
2. Use one suite at a time with B2 compile parallelism, for example:

   ```sh
   cd /Users/vishvananda/boost_1_91_0
   JOBS=12 ./run-cppgm-b2.sh -a libs/accumulators/test
   ```

3. If the suite passes, mark it in this tracker and continue to the next suite.
4. If it fails, identify the first real compiler frontier, reduce it to the
   smallest useful source, and prefer a non-STL reducer when possible.
5. Place the regression in the earliest owning PA and first correct cluster.
   Use `ROADMAP.md`, PA READMEs, the reducer's essential assertion, and:

   ```sh
   python3 scripts/audit_pa_feature_placement.py --pa paNN \
     --markdown-out /tmp/boost-frontier-placement-paNN.md \
     --json-out /tmp/boost-frontier-placement-paNN.json \
     --fail-on-early
   ```

   Include every touched PA. Course tests for PA1-PA9 belong under
   `cppgm.tests/course/pa*` unless the reducer is a hosted C++ extension, in
   which case use the hosted compatibility PAs.
6. Before implementation changes, confirm the reducer fails on the current
   branch and record the pre-fix diagnostic here.
7. Fix the compiler in `dev/`, validate the focused regression, the owner PA
   report, strict LowIR compare when relevant, and the Boost target.
8. Run the perf gate against the baseline above before committing any compiler
   fix.
9. Commit each coherent fix with the regression, implementation, and tracker
   update before moving to the next unrelated frontier.

## Fix Ledger

| Status | Frontier seed | Root cause | Owner PA | Regression | Pre-fix result | Validation | Fix commit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| fixed | Reset/revalidation `libs/any/test`; `basic_any_test` and `basic_any_test_rv` stack-overflowed while compiling `basic_tests<boost::anys::basic_any<8, 8>>::copy_counter::count`. | Reference static-member replay re-entered `ensure_class_reference_members` for a class whose reference members had already been collected while resolving the nested `copy_counter::count` member key. The replay path also did not mark out-of-class static member definitions as applied, so repeated member ensures could rescan the same reference definitions. The fix skips the ensure when reference members are already collected or collection is in progress, and marks reference replay applied once the stored definitions have been replayed. No text reparsing or fallback resolver is added. | `pa19` | `pa19/tests/general/100-reference-static-constexpr-member-replay.t` | Direct Boost compiles of `basic_any_test.cpp` and `basic_any_test_rv.cpp` exited `139`; the PA19 reducer exits `139` with the pre-fix `origin/main` compiler. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j4`; focused PA19 reducer passes; PA19 placement audit passes with `--fail-on-early`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa19' ... make test-report-nobuild` passes `114/114`; focused Boost `libs/any/test` passes and updates 164 targets; strict direct-LowIR compare passes PA18, PA19, PA21, PA22, and PA23; full direct-LowIR report passes `3290/3290`; text-reparse audit reports all zero; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-origin-main-3e469910-perf-baseline.json` passes (`+0.90%` instructions, RSS `-1.12%`, footprint `+0.03%`), report `/tmp/cppgm-boost-rebased-vs-origin-main-3e469910-perf-report-after-any-static-replay.json`. | this commit |
| fixed | Reset/revalidation `libs/algorithm/test//apply_permutation_test`; Boost.Test dependency build reached `boost::mp11::mp_eval_if` through `boost::iterators::iterator_facade` and `operator_arrow_dispatch` while compiling `libs/test/src/decorator.cpp`. | Structured alias type-id substitution replaced type and value parameters but left template-template parameter heads such as `F` in `mp_valid<F, T...>` intact inside `TemplateIdSyntax`. Nested alias expansion could then resolve a same-named pack from an outer alias context, so a concrete `mp_defer<facade_iterator_category_impl_t, forward_traversal_tag, basic_cstring, basic_cstring>` reused the earlier unrelated `mp_cond` pack. The fix substitutes template-template heads through structured template-id syntax and type-id ASTs, uses the alias target scope only when the dependent alias source arguments actually need it, and preserves alias dependent-defer caching to avoid the allocator-traits resolver churn seen in the first perf attempt. | `pa22:500` | `pa22/tests/general/500-template-template-alias-pack-scope-sfinae.t` | Direct Boost `decorator.cpp` failed with `ERROR: failed alias template type-id parse for mp_eval_if` and trace showed `mp_valid_impl` instantiated with the previous outer pack instead of the current `mp_defer` arguments. Focused B2 `libs/algorithm/test//apply_permutation_test` failed in Boost.Test dependency objects before reaching the later test-object frontier. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j12`; direct Boost `libs/test/src/decorator.cpp` compile passes; focused PA22 regression passes; PA22 placement audit passes with `--fail-on-early`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa22' ... make test-report-nobuild` passes `61/61`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 STRICT_PAS='pa18 pa19 pa21 pa22' ... make test-strict-nobuild` passes with compared counts PA18 `182`, PA19 `97`, PA21 `75`, PA22 `55`; focused B2 target updates 25 targets and now fails only the next `operator_arrow_dispatch` ABI-symbol frontier in `apply_permutation_test.o` and `framework.o`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-main-faed9c7fb-20260603.json` passes (`-0.52%` instructions, RSS `+0.83%`, footprint `+0.06%`). | this commit |
| fixed | Reset/revalidation `libs/algorithm/test//apply_permutation_test`; after the alias-pack fix, Boost.Test compilation reached `operator_arrow_dispatch<basic_cstring<char const>, basic_cstring<char const>*>::apply` while emitting host object symbols for `apply_permutation_test.o` and `framework.o`. | `function_binding_display_name_for_symbol` asked whether the whole qualified binding name was a conversion function. An owner class named `operator_arrow_dispatch` therefore made the ordinary static member `apply` look like a conversion operator returning nested `proxy`, and Itanium symbol construction tried to mangle `operator proxy` instead of `apply`. The fix strips the known owner-class prefix first, preserving real conversion-operator member names such as `operator N::Vec<T> &` without scanning owner components. | `pa32:200` | `pa32/tests/general/200-host-operator-prefix-template-owner-symbol.t` | Focused Boost compile failed with `failed to build ABI IR function symbol for weak function ... operator_arrow_dispatch<...>::operator ...::proxy`; the PA32 split-object reducer fails at implementation compile status `1` without the fix. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j12`; focused PA32 reducer passes and records `_ZN23operator_arrow_dispatchI6samplePS0_E5applyERKS0_` in both objects; PA32 placement audit passes with no placement findings; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa32' ... make test-report-nobuild` passes `78/78`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ... make test-strict-nobuild` passes PA18, PA19, PA21, PA22, and PA23 strict sets; direct Boost `libs/test/src/framework.cpp` compile and focused B2 `libs/algorithm/test//apply_permutation_test` advance to the next `boost::bind` overload-analysis frontier; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-main-faed9c7fb-20260603.json` passes (`-0.25%` instructions, RSS `-0.57%`, footprint `+0.08%`). | this commit |
| fixed | Reset/revalidation `libs/algorithm/test//apply_permutation_test`; after the operator-prefix ABI fix, Boost.Bind overload analysis reached `boost::_bi::list_av<bool (*)()>::type` while compiling `boost::bind(&impl::invoke_init_func, init_func)`. | Non-trivial type-pack expansions such as `typename add_value<A>::type...` only had a typed path when a value pack drove the expansion. | fixed | `libs/bloom/test//test_capacity`; `filter_core::capacity_for(100, 0.1)` evaluates `pow(1.0 - exp(-(double)2 / c), (double)2)` while the first argument is in `xmm1` and the second is in `xmm0`. | Native call lowering copied XMM arguments one at a time in argument order. For an argument shuffle such as `xmm1 -> xmm0` and `xmm0 -> xmm1`, the first copy clobbered the second source, so `pow` received the first argument twice and produced a huge capacity. The fix lowers XMM call arguments as a parallel copy, using a spare XMM register for cycles and a single 16-byte call scratch slot only when no spare exists; stack-passed float arguments and memory/immediate XMM arguments wait until their destination register is no longer needed as another argument source. | `pa32:200`; PA23 structural MIR refs updated for intentional call-setup order/no-op drift | `pa32/tests/general/200-host-f64-call-arg-shuffle.t`; PA23 structural refs `200-f64-direct-call`, `500-mixed-gpr-xmm-call-abi`, `600-indirect-mixed-gpr-xmm-call-abi`, and `700-call-setup-forwarding-no-preserve` | Focused reducer returned `direct_pow=0.694842` for the direct `pow` call while the stored-intermediate path returned `0.1`; Boost `test_capacity` aborted with uncaught `std::bad_alloc` after capacity overflow. | `make -C dev all -j12`; reducer now prints `direct=0.1 stored=0.1` and exits `0`; MIR dumps show scratch shuffling before `pow` and delayed memory-first materialization before a host `combine` call; focused PA32 regression passes; `ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa32' ... make test-report` passes `65/65`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa23' ... make test-report` passes `103/103`; focused `JOBS=12 ./run-cppgm-b2.sh -a libs/bloom/test//test_capacity` passes; full Bloom rerun advances to `test_construction` state mismatch and `test_fpr` long runtime/stall; full direct-LowIR report passes `3114/3114`; strict direct-LowIR compare passes; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json` passes (`+0.23%` instructions, RSS `+0.98%`, footprint `+1.37%`). | same commit |

A class whose copy construction is storage-trivial but whose selected move constructor is nontrivial was therefore lowered as `copyobj`, skipping source-state reset. The same copy-triviality shortcut was also used by synthesized move-constructor leading field-prefix copies. The fix adds a structured host-ABI move-triviality predicate and uses it for move constructor lifecycle/prefix decisions without reparsing source text or adding broad state tracking. | `pa16:300` | `pa16/tests/spec/300-defaulted-move-nontrivial-subobject.t`; direct-LowIR ref refreshes for `pa16/tests/spec/300-moveonly-defaulted-move.ref` and `pa18/tests/general/300-defaulted-move-constructor-reference-member.ref` after newly trivial defaulted moves lower directly to `copyobj`. | The reduced `stateful`/`empty_value`/`filter` probe returned failure because `f1.get().state` remained `42`; emitted LowIR for `filter(filter&&)` used `copyobj` for the `empty_value` base instead of calling the nontrivial `stateful(stateful&&)` base-entry constructor. Boost `test_construction` failed `f1.hash_function().state == -1` with observed state `42`. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j12`; reduced probe exits `0` and emitted LowIR calls the nontrivial base move path; focused PA16 regression passes; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa16' ... make test-report` passes `140/140`; PA16 placement audit exits cleanly with the new reducer owner-reached; focused `JOBS=12 ./run-cppgm-b2.sh -a libs/bloom/test//test_construction` passes; full `libs/bloom/test` passes and updates 38 targets; full direct-LowIR report passes `3115/3115`; strict direct-LowIR compare passes; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json` passes (`+0.28%` instructions, RSS `+1.17%`, footprint `+1.35%`). | this commit |
| fixed | `libs/charconv/test` `to_chars.o`; Boost.Dragonbox emits large `static constexpr` tables including `extended_cache_long_impl<true>::multiplier_index_info_table` and `main_cache_holder_impl<true>::cache`. | LowIR global array collection only handled scalar braced initializer data. Template-instantiated constexpr aggregate class arrays arrived as field assignment statements into `array[index].field`, and constexpr class arrays with user-provided constructors arrived as constructor actions. The fix collects both shapes from structured `CallSemNode` data: aggregate field assignments are sorted into static global data by byte offset, and simple constructor-action arrays are folded to static data by reading the resolved constructor body's `this->field = parameter/constant` assignments and substituting the call arguments. No source reparsing or broad tracking table is added, and large Boost constexpr tables avoid dynamic initializer calls. | `pa20:300` | `pa20/tests/spec/301-static-constexpr-aggregate-class-array-member.t`; `pa20/tests/spec/302-static-constexpr-constructor-class-array-member.t` | The aggregate reducer failed with `unsupported global array initializer`; after that path was covered, direct Boost `to_chars.cpp` advanced to `unsupported global array initializer for boost::charconv::detail::main_cache_holder_impl<true>::cache`. | fixed | `libs/chrono/test//implicit_constructot_fail` and `libs/chrono/test//time_point_implicit_f`; Boost.Chrono verifies that `duration` and `time_point` constructors marked `explicit` do not participate in ordinary copy-initialization. | Assignment-form class initialization only selected non-explicit constructors for copy-list-initialization; ordinary `T x = expr` still fell through to the direct-initialization profile and accepted explicit constructors. The fix uses the existing structured `initializer::uses_assignment_form` bit to select the non-explicit construction profile for ordinary copy-initialization, while preserving the same-type function-style prvalue path such as `T x = T(...)` by reusing the already computed constructor-argument shape. It adds no source reparsing, no broad tracking table, and avoids a repeated lookup on the class-initialization hot path. | `pa15:200` | `pa15/tests/general/200-copy-init-explicit-ctor-bad.t`; existing guards `pa15/tests/general/200-copy-list-init-explicit-ctor-bad.t`, `pa20/tests/general/300-constexpr-defaulted-constructor.t`, and `pa34/tests/compile/600-hosted-template-instantiation-compat.t`. | Direct Boost compile and B2 accepted the two compile-fail probes, so B2 reported skipped paired `.test` targets instead of `(failed-as-expected)` compile results. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j12`; focused PA15 reducer and existing copy-list guard pass; PA20 and PA34 materialization guards pass; PA15 placement audit passes with `--fail-on-early`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa15' ... make test-report-nobuild` passes `449/449`; strict direct-LowIR compare passes; focused B2 `implicit_constructot_fail` and `time_point_implicit_f` pass with `(failed-as-expected)` compile results; full `libs/chrono/test` passes with log `/tmp/boost-frontier-chrono-after-explicit-copy-init-20260527-final.log` and updates 420 targets; final three-run perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json` passes (`+0.26%` instructions, RSS `+0.41%`, footprint `+1.40%`). | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.Container `stable_vector_test`; `boost/container/detail/mutex.hpp` probes and calls GNU legacy sync-lock builtins. | Hosted builtin support did not advertise, analyze, or lower `__sync_lock_test_and_set` and `__sync_lock_release`, so Boost's lock wrapper fell through as unknown functions. The first perf check was narrowly over the instruction gate (`+1.02%`), so the builtin-call dispatcher now has a cheap double-underscore guard for ordinary non-builtin callees; this avoids a broad tracking table or source reparsing and brings the guarded implementation under the perf budget. | PA34 | `pa34/tests/compile/600-gnu-sync-lock-builtins.t` | Focused Boost build-only `stable_vector_test` stopped with `ERROR: unknown function __sync_lock_test_and_set` in `boost/container/detail/mutex.hpp`. | fixed | Boost.Container `scoped_allocator_adaptor_test`; unqualified explicit-template-id `get<I>(tuple)` found libc++'s declaration-only hidden friend through ADL but output had to emit the namespace `std::get` definition with its ABI tag. | Output binding resolution stopped at the ADL-selected friend declaration. That declaration had the right semantic namespace and template arguments but no body and no ABI tag, while the namespace-scope body template was not materialized unless the call was written as `std::get<I>(...)`. The fix is limited to declaration-only function-template bindings during output: require existing structured semantic qualified-name metadata, look for an already-defined same-name exact-type function, otherwise instantiate only same-shape function-template body declarations from that namespace with the already-known template arguments and then exact-type verify the materialized binding. | fixed | Boost.Container `devector_test`; Boost.Move must detect `noex_copy` as nothrow-copy-constructible so the test takes the nonthrowing copy path. | CPPGM did not treat `defined __is_identifier` as true and did not answer Clang-style `__is_identifier(__is_nothrow_constructible)` queries. Boost.Move therefore selected its weaker `__has_extension`-only trait detection path, missed the available `__is_nothrow_constructible` builtin, and fell back to `is_pod<T>::value`; `noex_copy` looked like a potentially throwing copy type and the runtime test deliberately threw. The fix implements Clang-compatible `__is_identifier` preprocessor query handling for keywords, ordinary identifiers, and supported type-trait builtins, and syncs the advertised `__has_builtin` type-trait names with the semantic builtin surface. It adds no source reparsing. | PA34:600 | `pa34/tests/compile/600-clang-is-identifier-type-trait-detection.t` | Probe `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260528/clang_defined_builtin_probe.cpp` failed because `defined __is_identifier` evaluated false under cppgm; Boost-shaped reducer `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260528/boost_move_noex_copy_trait.cpp` returned `1` under cppgm while clang returned `0`; focused Boost `libs/container/test//devector_test` terminated with uncaught `boost::container::test_exception`. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j8`; direct `clang_defined_builtin_probe.cpp` and `boost_move_noex_copy_trait.cpp` reducers pass; focused PA34 regression passes; host clang accepts the new PA34 fixture with `-x c++`; PA34 placement audit shows only pre-existing early-cluster findings and does not flag the new `600` test; full PA34 owner report is blocked by unrelated `700-hosted-nothrow-invocable-cache-default.t`, which also fails under host clang; default strict direct-LowIR compare passes; focused Boost `devector_test` passes; full Boost.Container runtime passes and updates 325 targets; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-7384ec2ae-20260528.json` passes: instructions `+0.19%`, RSS `+0.98%`, footprint `-0.07%`, log `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-is-identifier-check-20260528.log`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.Core `bit_countl_test`, `bit_floor_test`, `bit_width_test`, and the corresponding constexpr bitops targets for small unsigned operands. | Fixed GNU bit builtins `__builtin_clz`, `__builtin_ctz`, and `__builtin_popcount` were evaluated and lowered with the original argument width instead of their declared promoted unsigned parameter width; generic Clang `__builtin_*g` builtins keep the source operand width. `__builtin_clz((unsigned char)0x31)` therefore returned `2` instead of `26`, Boost subtracted the 24-bit promotion delta, and `bit_width` / `bit_floor` computed invalid results for small unsigned types. | fixed | Boost.Core `sp_thread_pause_test`, `yield_prim_pthread_cancel_test`, and `yield_prim_windows_h_test`; Boost.Core `yield_primitives.hpp` expands `sp_thread_pause()` to `__asm__ __volatile__("rep; nop" : : : "memory")` on this host. | Semantic analysis preserved only the GNU asm qualifiers and dropped the parsed asm clauses, while LowIR lowering rejected every `asm-statement`. | fixed | Boost.Core `underlying_type.run`; `boost::underlying_type<enum class E : unsigned short>::type` and the scoped-enum compatibility macro should expose the explicit enum base type. | `__underlying_type` accepted named enum types but always returned `int`. | fixed | Boost.DateTime `testwrapping_int`; `wrapping_int<short, 10>::subtract(-8)` computes both `v % wrap_val` and `-(v / wrap_val)` through signed 32-bit arithmetic before a member call. | Native object lowering fed `idivq` with narrow integer operands that had not been normalized to the 64-bit execution width. In object mode, loading an `i32` negative value into `%eax` zero-extended it to a positive 64-bit dividend before `cqto`, and a narrow negative divisor would have had the same problem in `%rcx`. The fix normalizes both `%rax` and `%rcx` from the structured LowIR type immediately before signed or unsigned division/modulo. It adds no source or type reparsing. | PA32 host object/runtime | `pa32/tests/general/200-signed-i32-divmod-object-runtime-smoke.t` | Minimal object-mode reducer `scratch/member_call_after_mod_no_store_reducer.cpp` matched clang with exit `1` only after the fix; before the fix clang exited `1` while cppgm exited `205`. | fixed | Boost.DateTime `testgreg_month`; `greg_month::as_short_string()` and `as_long_string()` define function-local `static const char * const names[NumMonths]` arrays with one omitted pointer element. | Synthesized zero-fill pointer elements from array initialization reached global/static LowIR data emission as literal pointer-typed `nullptr` nodes. The global initializer code recognized `nullptr_t` and integral zero, but not pointer-typed literal `nullptr`, so it printed `ptr nullptr`; the LowIR parser requires `ptr addr @symbol` for pointer data and rejects null pointer data unless it is emitted as `zero 8`. | fixed | Boost.Coroutine `test_asymmetric_coroutine.cpp` and `test_symmetric_coroutine.cpp`; coroutine tests redeclare `coro::...::iterator` after a local object named `coro`. | The parser's declaration-start heuristic rejected named decl-specifier candidates when the first identifier had a nearer value binding. In Boost.Coroutine, the first qualifier is also a namespace alias, and `coro::...` remains a valid nested-name-specifier even after declaring a local variable named `coro`. The fix allows `identifier::` to start a qualified type/namespace candidate regardless of value-name preference, while keeping the `identifier<...>` template-id ambiguity guarded by the existing value/type heuristic. No source reparsing was added. | PA10 parser | `pa10/tests/general/301-namespace-alias-shadow-qualified-type.t` | Fresh Boost.Coroutine direct run failed with `expected declaration near KW_VOID:void` at `test_asymmetric_coroutine.cpp:458` and `test_symmetric_coroutine.cpp:393`; a reduced PA10 probe fails before the fix when `alias::duration` follows a local object named `alias`. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j8`; focused PA10 regression passes and clang accepts it; PA10 placement audit still exits nonzero on pre-existing PA10 syntax-suite findings but does not list this reducer; `CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa10' ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=12 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 make test-report-nobuild` passes `130/130`; strict direct-LowIR compare passes with PA18 `136/0`, PA19 `93/0`, PA21 `168/0`, PA22 `389/0`; focused Boost `libs/coroutine/test//test_asymmetric_coroutine` advances past the parse frontier to the next Boost.Exception safe-bool member-pointer cv conversion failure, log `/tmp/boost-frontier-coroutine-asymmetric-after-qualified-shadow-20260528.log`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-7384ec2ae-20260528.json` passes: instructions `-0.07%`, RSS `-0.65%`, footprint `+0.46%`, log `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-qualified-shadow-check-20260528.log`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.Coroutine `test_asymmetric_coroutine.cpp`; Boost.Exception safe-bool returns `ptr_ ? &impl::get : 0`, where `impl::get` has type `T* get() const` and the target typedef is `T* (impl::*)() const`. | Declarator CV association checked pending pointer prefixes before pending function suffixes. For a member-function pointer returning a pointer, `D const * (C::*)() const` attached the trailing `const` to the return pointer instead of to the function suffix, storing a target type shaped as `function of () returning const pointer` instead of `function of () const returning pointer`. The fix gives an already-parsed function suffix priority for following cv-qualifiers; pointer cv still applies when the qualifier appears immediately after the pointer operator before any function suffix. | PA12 declarator/type semantics | `pa12/tests/general/300-member-function-pointer-return-pointer-const.t` | Focused Boost target failed with `invalid return expression [target member-pointer ... function of () returning const pointer ...] [expr_type member-pointer ... function of () const returning pointer ...]` in `boost/exception/detail/exception_ptr.hpp:110`; non-STL reducer `scratch/safe_bool_member_ptr_reduce.cpp` reproduced the same mismatch before the fix, while clang accepted it. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j8`; focused PA12 reducer and existing `300-member-function-pointer-type-alias-and-function.t` pass; clang accepts the reducer; PA12 placement audit exits nonzero on pre-existing PA12 findings and stale member-pointer owner rules that already flag the older PA12 member-pointer tests; PA12 full report remains blocked by three pre-existing reference drifts (`200-local-alias-postfix-cv-declaration.t`, `200-local-anonymous-union-variable.t`, `300-block-anonymous-union-injected-members.t`), confirmed with this source change reverted; strict direct-LowIR compare passes with PA18 `136/0`, PA19 `93/0`, PA21 `168/0`, PA22 `389/0`; focused Boost `libs/coroutine/test//test_asymmetric_coroutine` advances to the `push_coroutine_object` missing LowIR declaration frontier, log `/tmp/boost-frontier-coroutine-asymmetric-after-member-fnptr-return-pointer-20260529.log`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-7384ec2ae-20260528.json` passes: instructions `-0.23%`, RSS `+1.37%`, footprint `+0.46%`, log `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-member-fnptr-return-pointer-check-20260529.log`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.Core `uncaught_exceptions.run` and `uncaught_exceptions_np.run`; destructors reached while unwinding from `throw Ex()` must observe the host ABI uncaught-exception count after `__cxa_throw` starts. | Host throw lowering emitted ordinary scope cleanups immediately before calling `__cxa_throw`, so local destructors ran before the Itanium runtime incremented the uncaught-exception count. The fix keeps the already-built structured cleanup stack and opens the existing host unwind cleanup region around the final `__cxa_throw` call, letting the landing pad run registered destructors after the throw begins. Constructor-unwind dispatch metadata now follows the shared host cleanup region when needed. No source reparsing was added. | PA32 host exception lowering | `pa32/tests/general/200-host-eh-throw-dtor-sees-uncaught-count.t` | The PA32 reducer returned `1` before the fix because the destructor saw the same count as the root frame. Focused Boost `uncaught_exceptions.run` reported `root_count != level1_count` as `0 != 0`, and `uncaught_exceptions_np.run` reported all nested counts as `0`. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j8`; focused PA32 reducer passes and clang accepts it; LowIR shows `Watcher::~Watcher` in `call_unwind_dispatch` after the throwing call starts; related PA32 host-EH guards for rethrow, unmatched call cleanup, call-argument temporary cleanup, same-TU catch, and unwind cleanup pass; focused Boost `libs/core/test//uncaught_exceptions` and `libs/core/test//uncaught_exceptions_np` pass, log `/tmp/boost-frontier-core-uncaught-exceptions-after-host-throw-cleanup-20260528.log`; PA32 placement audit passes; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa32' ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=12 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 make test-report-nobuild` passes `86/86`; strict direct-LowIR compare passes; full Boost.Core rerun reports both uncaught targets passing and `failed updating 0 target`, with B2 still returning nonzero because it reports skipped expected-fail dependency targets, log `/tmp/boost-frontier-core-after-host-throw-cleanup-20260528.log`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-7384ec2ae-20260528.json` passes: instructions `-0.22%`, RSS `+2.01%`, footprint `+0.47%`, log `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-host-throw-cleanup-check-20260528.log`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.Core `underlying_type.run`; `boost::underlying_type<enum class E : unsigned short>::type` and the scoped-enum compatibility macro should expose the explicit enum base type. | `__underlying_type` accepted named enum types but always returned `int`. Enum parsing already computed size/alignment from the explicit base type, but discarded the structured base `TypePtr`, so hosted type traits could not recover `unsigned char` / `unsigned short` without reparsing. The fix stores the parsed enum underlying type on the named enum type and returns it from `__underlying_type`, falling back to `int` only when no metadata is present. Builtin `std::__1::align_val_t` also records its `size_t` base. No source reparsing was added. | PA34 hosted compiler transform compatibility, backed by earlier enum metadata | `pa34/tests/compile/500-underlying-type-explicit-enum-base.t` | Focused Boost `underlying_type.run` reported `boost::is_same<int, unsigned char>` and `boost::is_same<int, unsigned short>` failures. The reduced PA34 test failed before the fix with `ScopedCharBase=int`, `ScopedShortBase=int`, and `PlainCharBase=int`. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j8`; focused PA34 reducer passes and clang accepts it; focused Boost `libs/core/test//underlying_type` passes, log `/tmp/boost-frontier-core-underlying-type-after-enum-underlying-20260528.log`; PA34 placement audit reports only pre-existing PA34 cluster-early findings and not the new reducer; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa16 pa34' ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=12 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=2 make test-report-nobuild` passes `1345/1345`; strict direct-LowIR compare passes; full Boost.Core rerun now leaves only `uncaught_exceptions.run` and `uncaught_exceptions_np.run`, log `/tmp/boost-frontier-core-after-enum-underlying-type-20260528.log`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-7384ec2ae-20260528.json` passes: instructions `-0.05%`, RSS `+0.86%`, footprint `+0.45%`, log `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-enum-underlying-type-check-20260528.log`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.Coroutine `test_asymmetric_coroutine.cpp`; `push_coroutine_object` instantiates polymorphic class-template specializations whose type arguments include both `int *` and `int &`. | LowIR internal symbols for vtables, RTTI, and typeinfo-name globals were derived from sanitized display names. The sanitizer collapsed punctuation-sensitive template arguments, so `T=int *` and `T=int &` shared names such as `@...int_____vtable` even though their host object symbols were distinct. The object backend later saw a vtable entry referencing the overwritten specialization's destructor symbol without a matching LowIR declaration. The fix switches only structurally-sensitive type surfaces to Itanium type-encoding internal symbols, using existing template argument/type metadata and adding no source reparsing. | PA32 host ABI vtable/RTTI symbol ownership; PA18 LowIR ref refresh for an existing array/reference template-vtable case | `pa32/tests/general/200-host-template-ptr-ref-vtable-symbols.t`; refreshed `pa18/tests/general/300-template-array-reference-cv-default-arg.ref` for the intentional internal symbol spelling change | Focused reducer `scratch/vtable_ptr_ref_collision.cpp` failed before the fix with `ERROR: undefined symbol lacks LowIR declaration @derived_int______derived`; Boost `test_asymmetric_coroutine.o` failed on the analogous `push_coroutine_object` destructor symbol. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j8`; focused PA32 regression passes; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa32' ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=12 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 make test-report-nobuild` passes `87/87`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 STRICT_PAS='pa18 pa19 pa21 pa22' STRICT_SUBTEST_JOBS=12 make test-strict-nobuild` passes; focused Boost `libs/coroutine/test//test_asymmetric_coroutine` builds `test_asymmetric_coroutine.o` and advances to the known Boost.Regex `char_syntax` dependency failure, log `/tmp/boost-frontier-coroutine-asymmetric-after-vtable-type-symbols-20260529.log`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-7384ec2ae-20260528.json` passes: instructions `-0.36%`, RSS `+0.30%`, footprint `+0.46%`, log `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-vtable-type-symbols-check-20260529.log`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.Regex dependency for Boost.Coroutine `test_asymmetric_coroutine` / `test_symmetric_coroutine`; `get_default_syntax_type` and `get_default_escape_syntax_type` initialize `static ... char_syntax[]` from namespace-scope `static const unsigned char` constants. | Ordinary scalar constant variable id-expressions correctly stayed lvalues after the earlier constant-folding cleanup, but LowIR global array initializer lowering only accepted literals and address forms. | fixed | Boost.CRC `quick`, `pr15_test`, and `crc_test`; `make_partial_xor_products_table` initializes `std::array<Register, N> result = { 0 };`. | Positional aggregate initialization only synthesized brace-elided sublists for aggregate or array fields when they consumed more than one initializer clause. | fixed | Boost.Core `type_name_test`; `std::map<...>` and `std::unordered_map<...>` should print without their default comparator/hash/equality/allocator arguments. | Class partial-specialization matching decomposed the actual class-template-id through source argument text when available, so `map<int, value>` exposed only two explicit arguments even though the instantiated type carried four defaulted arguments. | fixed | Boost.Core `type_name_test`; `type_name<void() &>`, `type_name<void() const &>`, pack-expanded class names, function argument class names, and cv-qualified pointer partials must keep their structured type identity. | Three type-name frontiers were masking each other. Class partial-specialization matching stripped top-level cv from actual composite types even when the pattern did not spell cv, so `holder<T*>` could match `B const* volatile` and tie a cv-qualified pointer partial. Pack expansion for explicit function-template type arguments cloned only text, so `add_each<T>()...` could resolve the printed `A` through a local typedef instead of the already-bound class type. Finally, the structured function type model carried function cv but not `&` / `&&`, so `holder<void() &>` collapsed to `holder<void>`. The fix preserves actual cv during class-partial deduction, carries `resolved_type` through cloned and synthesized pack argument syntax, makes the fast explicit-argument resolver prefer that structured type, and adds a ref-qualifier field to function types, spelling, equality, hashing, template matching, and mangle keys. | fixed | Boost.ContainerHash `hash_integral_test`; `boost::is_signed<wchar_t>` should be true on this host and `boost::is_signed<char32_t>` should be false, so the identity hash test chooses the same signed/unsigned branch as clang. | CPPGM modeled `wchar_t` as an unsigned 32-bit fundamental even though host clang reports `__WCHAR_TYPE__ int`, and the constexpr evaluator plus semantic conversion helper promoted `char32_t` to `int` even though `int` cannot represent all `char32_t` values. Boost.TypeTraits therefore computed the wrong signedness for both wide character types: `wchar_t` hashed through an unsigned conversion while the test expected signed `size_t` conversion, and `char32_t` was classified as signed by `(char32_t)-1 > (char32_t)0` evaluating false in constexpr. The fix makes the built-in `wchar_t` model signed, removes it from unsigned integral traits, and keeps the semantic and constexpr integral-promotion helpers aligned for `wchar_t` and `char32_t`. It uses existing type metadata with no source reparsing. | PA19:100 | `pa19/tests/general/100-char32-constant-promotion-unsigned.t` | Focused reducer `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260528/char32_unsigned_promotion.cpp` failed its `static_assert` under cppgm while clang accepted it; full ContainerHash runtime failed only `hash_integral_test.run` with 256 `char32_t` identity mismatches, then focused rerun after the first promotion fix failed 128 `wchar_t` identity mismatches. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j8`; reducer and clang control pass; focused PA19 regression passes; PA19 placement audit passes with `--fail-on-early`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa19' ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=8 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 make test-report-nobuild` passes `111/111`; default strict direct-LowIR compare passes; focused Boost `libs/container_hash/test//hash_integral_test` passes; full Boost.ContainerHash runtime passes and updates 319 targets; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-7384ec2ae-20260528.json` passes: instructions `+0.19%`, RSS `+0.06%`, footprint `-0.02%`, log `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-wchar-char32-promotion-check-20260528.log`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.Container `map_test`; `boost::container::test::expected_pair[i]` placement-new writes in the emplace checks must write into the pointed-to `pair_storage`, not into the `expected_pair` global variable itself. | LowIR array-base lowering treated every global id-expression as an array object and emitted `addr @global` plus decay. For global pointer variables, `p[i]` therefore indexed from the storage address of the pointer object instead of the pointer value loaded from that object. In `map_test`, writes through `expected_pair[i]` overwrote the `expected_pair` pointer and following globals, corrupting the adaptive-pool singleton state before process teardown. The fix routes global subscript bases through the existing structured rvalue path, so global arrays still decay to storage, global pointers load their stored pointer, and references keep their existing behavior. It adds no source reparsing, persistent tracking table, or broad side state. | PA14 | `pa14/tests/general/200-global-pointer-array-subscript-load.t` | Scalar reducer `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260528/global_pointer_subscript_scalar.cpp` returned `1` under cppgm while clang returned `0`; pre-fix LowIR showed `addr @cursor` / `unary decay ptr` for `cursor[1]`. Boost-shaped reducer `/tmp/cppgm-boost-reducers-20260528/map_after_adaptive_expected_only.cpp` wrote through `addr @expected_pair`, and focused Boost `libs/container/test//map_test` aborted in `adaptive_node_pool_impl.hpp:959` with `n_free_nodes == m_totally_free_blocks`. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j8`; scalar and Boost-shaped reducers pass, and the Boost-shaped LowIR now loads `@boost__container__test__expected_pair` before indexing; focused PA14 regression passes; PA14 placement audit passes with `--fail-on-early`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa14' ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=8 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 make test-report-nobuild` passes `60/60`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 STRICT_SUBTEST_JOBS=8 make test-strict-nobuild` passes; focused Boost `libs/container/test//map_test` passes; full Container runtime now fails only `devector_test.run`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-9584a3460-20260528.json` passes: instructions `-0.03%`, RSS `+0.80%`, footprint `+0.01%`, log `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-global-pointer-subscript-check-20260528.log`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.ContainerHash `libs/container_hash/examples/template.cpp`; `prime_fmod_size<void>::inv_sizes32` and `prime_fmod_size<void>::positions` are odr-used from `prime_fmod_size<void>::position(hash, size_index)` and require emitted out-of-class static data definitions. | The template declaration collector classified `constexpr std::size_t (*prime_fmod_size<T>::positions[])(std::size_t);` as an out-of-class member function because it treated any descendant `parameter-clause` as method-like, including the pointee function signature inside `(*positions[])(...)`. After that was corrected, early reference-member collection could still miss `positions`, mark the class template's stored static definitions as applied, and prevent the existing later static-member use path from retrying. The fix uses a cheap AST-shape test for whether a declarator's parameter clause belongs to the declared entity, and leaves stored out-of-class static definitions retryable only when a member is missing before class completion. It adds no source reparsing, global readiness table, or broad static-member scan. | PA20 | `pa20/tests/general/300-static-constexpr-function-template-pointer-array.t` | Reduced `prime_fmod_size` case `/tmp/cppgm-prime-fmod-static-members.cpp` emitted a definition for `inv_sizes32` but only `declare global @prime_fmod_size_void___positions`, then failed link with undefined `prime_fmod_size<void>::positions`; focused Boost example failed link with undefined `prime_fmod_size<void>::inv_sizes32` and `prime_fmod_size<void>::positions`. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j4`; trusted-inode carrier used for local executable launch; focused PA20 regression passes and now defines `inv_sizes32`, `positions`, and `sizes`; PA20 placement audit passes with `--fail-on-early`; reduced `prime_fmod_size` case compiles and links; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa20' ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=8 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 make test-report-nobuild` passes `78/78`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 STRICT_PAS='pa18 pa19 pa21 pa22' STRICT_SUBTEST_JOBS=8 make test-strict-nobuild` passes; focused Boost `libs/container_hash/examples//template testing.execute=off` passes and `nm -C` shows `D` definitions for `sizes`, `inv_sizes32`, and `positions`; full ContainerHash build-only passes and updates 163 targets, log `/tmp/boost-frontier-container-hash-buildonly-after-static-member-definitions-20260528.log`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-257413d6f-20260527.json` passes: instructions `-0.16%`, RSS `-0.17%`, footprint `+0.57%`, report `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-static-member-definition-retry-20260528.json`. | this commit |
| fixed | Boost.ContainerHash `libs/container_hash/examples/template.cpp`; `prime_fmod_size<void>::positions` is a `constexpr static` array of function pointers initialized with function template-ids such as `position<29, sizes[29]>`. | Typed constexpr initialization of function-pointer array elements only handled explicit `&function` addresses, so direct function designators could fall through to ordinary constant-expression evaluation. For the template-id case, the parser also preserved `sizes[N]` as only an ambiguous type-id form in template arguments, so non-type template argument resolution could not structurally evaluate the static constexpr array subscript. The fix accepts direct function designators only when the target type is a function pointer, and preserves both type and expression syntax for ambiguous `name[...]` template arguments so non-type resolution can use the expression AST. | fixed | Boost.Container `scoped_allocator_adaptor_test`; `dtl::dispatch_uses_allocator(..., piecewise_construct_t, boost::tuples::tuple<int, null_type, ...>, boost::tuples::tuple<int, null_type, ...>)` should select the fixed Boost.Tuple overload rather than tie with the variadic template-template fallback. | Function-template partial ordering did not build a usable transformed placeholder for template-template parameters, and substitution of a dependent template-template replacement did not preserve structured argument metadata. | fixed | Boost.Container `small_vector_test` via Boost.Intrusive `pointer_rebinder<Ptr<A, An...>, U, 0u>` versus fixed-arity `pointer_rebinder<Ptr<A>, U, 0u>` | Partial-specialization ordering treated a direct template-template pattern with a trailing template-argument pack as equally specialized with the fixed-arity pattern when the pack matched empty. The fix compares existing parsed `TemplateIdSyntax`/`TemplateArgumentSyntax` for direct template-template heads and prefers the fixed prefix when the other pattern is exactly the same prefix plus a trailing pack suffix. The tiebreaker is guarded by cheap template-template/pack checks, so ordinary partial-specialization ties avoid the structured scan; it adds no source reparsing and no persistent tracking table. | PA21 | `pa21/tests/general/400-template-template-fixed-prefix-pack-order.t` | Minimal reducer failed with `ERROR: ambiguous partial class specialization` for `pointer_rebinder<alloc<int>, void, 0u>`; focused Boost `libs/container/test//small_vector_test` failed at the same Boost.Intrusive `pointer_rebinder` path before reaching `vector::prot_swap_small`. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j4`; trusted-inode compiler smoke passed; focused PA21 reducer passes; placement audit for PA21 passes with `--fail-on-early`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa21' ORDERED=false TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=8 make test-report-nobuild` passes `193/193`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 STRICT_SUBTEST_JOBS=8 make test-strict-nobuild` passes all strict PAs; focused Boost build-only `small_vector_test` advances to the separate `prot_swap_small` invalid-initializer frontier, and `stable_vector_test` advances to the separate `__sync_lock_test_and_set` builtin frontier; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json` passes after guard tightening: instructions `+0.69%`, RSS `+1.21%`, footprint `+1.39%`, report `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-template-template-fixed-prefix-pack-guarded-20260527.json`. | this commit |
| fixed | `libs/circular_buffer/test//base_test_dbg` and `//space_optimized_test_dbg`; debug iterators are passed through condition calls, short-circuit RHS checks, and `assign_n(..., cb_details::make_assign_range(...))` as class temporaries. | Three temporary-lifetime holes combined in the debug iterator paths. Simple branch conditions did not lower inside a full-expression cleanup scope, so class temporaries used by condition calls could survive into the selected branch. The short-circuit RHS path checked for cleanups before emitting the RHS expression, so RHS temporaries were not destroyed before the true/false edge. Finally, output support for prvalue class temporaries bound to reference parameters only required a destructor when the class type already had layout; Boost's template `assign_range<iterator, allocator>` was known structurally but not yet layout-complete during collection, so LowIR could materialize the hidden reference argument without an `assign_range` destructor symbol or cleanup call. The fix reuses existing cleanup scopes and completes only the structured class type for non-lvalue, non-reference arguments bound to reference parameters; it adds no source reparsing or broad lifetime tracker. | `pa16:300`; `pa18:300` for the template-shaped reference-bound reducer | `pa16/tests/general/300-condition-call-argument-temporary-cleanup.t`; `pa16/tests/general/300-short-circuit-condition-rhs-temp-cleanup.t`; `pa18/tests/general/300-reference-prvalue-template-member-temp-cleanup.t` | Focused no-STL reducers returned live leaked temporaries, and the reference-bound template reducer exits `2` when the old layout-complete predicate is restored. Direct Boost-shaped `circular_base_dbg_assign_iterator_probe.cpp` segfaulted with exit `139`; generated LowIR constructed the `assign_range` hidden argument but did not emit its destructor, leaving stale debug iterators registered. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j4`; focused PA16/PA18 regressions pass and their native executables exit `0`; placement audits for PA16 and PA18 have no new early-owner findings; the reference-bound reducer fails before and passes after the final structured-completion fix; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa16' ... make test-report-nobuild` passes `144/144`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa18' ... make test-report-nobuild` passes `171/171`; strict direct-LowIR compare passes; focused Boost `base_test_dbg` and `space_optimized_test_dbg` pass; full `JOBS=12 ./run-cppgm-b2.sh -a libs/circular_buffer/test` passes and updates 24 targets; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json` passes (`+0.51%` instructions, RSS `+2.70%`, footprint `+1.35%`). | this commit |
| fixed | `libs/circular_buffer/test//base_test` and `//space_optimized_test`; `boost::circular_buffer::at(0)` threw `boost::wrapexcept<std::out_of_range>` immediately after one `push_back`. | Direct-branch load elision rewrote a one-use load from a promoted parameter slot into a later compare source. When the load-to-branch range crossed a call, the compare read the forwarded parameter's caller-saved register after the call had clobbered it, so `check_position(0)` compared a pointer-like clobbered value against `size() == 1`. The fix keeps the existing elision fast path but declines it only when a promoted parameter-slot load would cross a caller-saved clobber before the branch, causing the loaded value to materialize in the normal temp interval and use a callee-saved register or frame slot. It adds no source reparsing and only a local instruction-position scan during machine-layout collection. | `pa23:structural` | `pa23/tests/structural/800-promoted-param-slot-direct-branch-call-clobber.t` | PA23 LowIR reducer `scratch/pa23_call_slot_bypass_reduce.lowir` exited `3`; no-STL C++ reducer `scratch/debug_info_void_check_reduce.cpp` exited `3` with `./dev/cppgm++ -std=c++11 -g`; focused B2 `base_test` aborted in `at_test()` with `out_of_range`. | `make -C dev lowir2native cppgm++ -j4`; PA23 LowIR reducer now exits `0`; no-STL C++ reducer with `-g` exits `0`; focused PA23 regression passes; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa23' ... make test-report` passes `104/104`; focused B2 `base_test` and `space_optimized_test` pass; `base_test_dbg` remains the next debug-mode segfault frontier; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json` passes (`+0.13%` instructions, RSS `+0.28%`, footprint `+1.41%`). | this commit |

Materialized class temporaries passed as constructor arguments were registered only when a full-expression scope was active, so the `InstanceCounter()` argument survived until function exit and left one extra live object before `erase_begin`, `erase_end`, and `clear`. The fix wraps class-variable constructor-action lowering in the existing full-expression cleanup scope and reuses existing temporary cleanup registration; it adds no source reparsing or new tracking structure. | `pa16:200` | `pa16/tests/general/200-constructor-argument-temporary-dtor.t`; existing `pa16/tests/general/300-conditional-local-prvalue-init-elides-copy.t` reference refreshed to include the newly emitted conditional-prvalue temporary destructor. | Focused B2 failed six `InstanceCounter::count()` checks in `constant_erase_test`; no-STL reducer `scratch/temporary_argument_lifetime_reduce.cpp` exited `6` with cppgm and `0` with clang. | fixed | `libs/chrono/test//duration_input_h.test` and `libs/chrono/test//v1_duration_input_h.test`; Boost.Chrono `duration_get<...>::get` computes a `long double` intermediate and divides it by an unsigned integral period denominator. | Compound-assignment LowIR lowering used the LHS/result LowIR type for the binary operation but fed the raw RHS storage into the op. For `long double /= unsigned long long`, this emitted `binary div f80 ..., %i64`, and native lowering rejected the integer temp as unknown f80 storage. The fix derives the compound operand type from existing semantic operand types for arithmetic, integral, and shift assignments, converts both operands before the binary op, then converts the raw result back to the LHS storage type. This is on-demand expression lowering with no source reparsing and no persistent tracking table. | `pa14:200` | `pa14/tests/general/200-floating-compound-assign-integral-rhs.t`; existing `pa14/tests/general/100-condition-declaration-variable-rvalue.ref` refreshed because `n += unsigned char` now zero-extends the RHS before the `i32` add. | The direct reducer lowered to `binary div f80` with an `i64` RHS and `lowir2native` failed with `ERROR: unknown storage %t2 in @main`; Boost `duration_input_h` / `v1_duration_input_h` failed similarly with unknown `%t220` / `%t364` storage in generated duration parsing code. | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j12`; focused PA14 reducer passes; PA14 placement audit passes with `--fail-on-early`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS='pa14' ... make test-report-nobuild` passes `56/56`; strict direct-LowIR compare passes; focused B2 `libs/chrono/test//duration_input_h` and `//v1_duration_input_h` pass; full `libs/chrono/test` advances to 3 failed updates with log `/tmp/boost-frontier-chrono-after-compound-assign-20260527.log`; three-run perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-baseline-boost-frontier-current-local-gate-20260525.json` passes (`+0.23%` instructions, RSS `+1.59%`, footprint `+1.37%`). | this commit |
| fixed | `libs/chrono/test//rounding_pass_h.test`; `boost::chrono::detail::duration_units_default_initializer_t<char>::duration_units_default_initializer_t` allocates dynamic arrays of nontrivial `std::basic_string<char>` with `new string_type[3]` and `new string_type[19 * 3]`. | Class array `new` still rejected nontrivially destructible element types and had no element-count cookie for class array `delete[]`. The fix keeps the data local to the allocation: class array allocation reserves a compact cookie before the returned object pointer, stores the structured element count, and marked class array delete lowers to a separate path that subtracts the cookie, destroys elements in reverse order when a destructor exists, and frees the original allocation pointer. | fixed | `libs/chrono/test//cycle_count_h.test`; Boost.MPL `and_` default-argument binding materializes `boost::integral_constant<bool, ::boost::detail::is_convertible_impl_dispatch<From, To>::value>` while checking `duration<double, boost::nano>(clock::duration(1))`. | The fast existing class-template lookup path cleared output state at entry, then appended each resolved argument. | committed | `libs/asio/test//cancel_at`; nested cancellation tokens eventually instantiate `async_result<cancel_at_t<...>>::initiate` with dependent trailing-return calls that forward `Args&&...` through `static_cast<Args&&>(args)...`. | Substituting a direct template type-id with a reference declarator built synthetic type-id nodes whose reference target collapsed into reparsed/display text such as `int &&` instead of structured `type_id` plus `abstract_declarator`. Later `static_cast` analysis could then miss the substituted semantic type and re-enter textual parsing. The fix carries the substituted reference type structurally, applies simple `&` and `&&` declarators to direct type-id substitutions with normal reference collapsing, and lets cast analysis consume the attached nondependent semantic type before falling back to parsed type-id handling.
